Line search with Adam:
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_sequence(a, time_budget, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    x = x - x.max()  # keep <=0
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
    lr=0.2; momentum=0.9
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    beta=20.0
    stale=0
    last_best=best_score
    while time<deadline:
       # inner loop over beta schedule? We can increase beta slowly every 500 iters.
       for _ in range(100):
          a = exp(x) (stable)
          c = conv_self(a); cmax=c.max(); if cmax<=0 break; scale=1/sqrt(cmax); x += log(scale); a*=scale; c*=scale? c/=cmax; S=a.sum()
          # F
          shifted=beta*(c-1)
          shifted -= shifted.max()  # max 0
          sumexp=np.exp(shifted).sum()
          smooth=1 + np.log(sumexp)/beta
          F=smooth - 2*np.log(S)
          # p
          p=np.exp(shifted); p/=sumexp
          E=np.dot(p,c)
          g=conv_cross(a,p)
          grad=2*a*((g - E) - 1/S)
          # if S too small? inf
          # adapt beta: every 200 iters beta*=1.1 up to 500
          # Adam update
          ...
          # propose x_new = x - lr * calibration
          # line search maybe 5 steps factor 0.5 if F worse
          # if accepted and exact score improved update best
       # occasionally perturb x with noise or random restart from analytic
    return best
```

Let's detail `optimize_fixed_length(a_init, time_budget, seed, target=None)`.
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_fixed_length(a0, time_budget, seed=0, beta0=50.0, target_score=None):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = np.asarray(a0,dtype=float).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: a=np.ones_like(a); S=1
    a = a/S
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
    deadline=time.time()+time_budget
    x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    x -= np.max(x)
    # For length, choose beta0 maybe 100? Adaptive.
    beta0 = 50.0
    lr_target=0.1
    mom=0.85
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    stale=0
    last_best=best_score
    iter_count=0
    beta0_current=20.0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # maybe increase beta0 every 200 iterations
       a = np.exp(x - np.max(x))
       a /= a.sum()
       c = conv_self(a)
       C = np.max(c)
       if not finite or C<=0: break
       # smooth estimate
       t = beta0_current * (c - C)/max(C,1e-300)
       t -= t.max()
       sumexp = np.exp(t).sum()
       W = C + (C / beta0_current) * np.log(sumexp)
       # if W better than best_score/(2n) update? exact later
       # p and gradient
       p = np.exp(t); p /= sumexp
       E = np.dot(p,c)
       g = cross_corr(a,p,n)
       base = 2.0 * a * (g - E)
       # add a small centering to gradient? Since softmax, sum base? We can subtract weighted mean to keep scale? Not necessary.
       # Adam
       m = mom*m + (1-mom)*base
       v = mom*v + (1-mom)*base*base
       m_hat = m/(1-mom**iter_count)
       v_hat = v/(1-mom**iter_count)
       step = m_hat/(np.sqrt(v_hat)+1e-12)
       max_step = np.max(np.abs(step))
       if max_step>0:
           scale = lr_target / max_step
       else: scale=1
       # maybe cap scale to avoid huge due to tiny grad? If max_step < 1e-12, scale=1
       step = step * scale
       # line search
       accepted=False
       f_cur = W
       for ls in range(6):
           x_try = x - step
           x_try -= np.max(x_try)  # scale invariance, but softmax sum fixed anyway; exp stable
           f_try = smooth_score_from_x(x_try, beta0_current)  # returns W, maybe S? But need a/c.
           if f_try < f_cur - 1e-14 or ls==0 and f_try < f_cur:
               x=x_try; W=f_try; accepted=True; break
           step *= 0.5
       if accepted:
          # periodic exact
          if iter_count % 20 ==0:
             a_try=np.exp(x - x.max()); a_try/=a_try.sum()
             sc=fast_score(a_try)
             if sc < best_score:
                 best=a_try.copy(); best_score=sc; stale=0
             else: stale += 1
          # adaptive beta0 and lr
          iter_count +=1
       else:
          # maybe reset momentum, smaller lr target
          lr_target *=0.7
          m=m*0.5; v*=0.5
          stale +=1
       # if stale high, perturb or restart
       if stale > 200:
           # random perturbation proportional to x scale
           x += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
           x -= np.max(x)
           lr_target = max(0.05, lr_target*0.5)
           stale=0
       # increase beta0
       if iter_count % 100 ==0 and beta0_current < 2000:
           beta0_current *=1.2
       if target_score and best_score <= target_score:
           break
    # final exact
    a = np.exp(x - x.max()); a/=a.sum(); sc=fast_score(a); if sc<best_score...
    return best, best_score
```

We can precompute FFT sizes and use `np.fft.rfft` local variables for speed. Cross `g`:
```
def cross_corr_needed(a,p,n,size_g):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size_g)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size_g)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fp, size_g)  # conv a*p? Need g = sum_i a[i] p[i+j]
```
If conv = a * p linear, conv[j+i]? Let's derive with orientation. We want g[j]=∑_i a[i] p[i+j]. If conv = a * p: conv[m]=∑_i a[i] p[m-i]. Need m-i = i+j -> no. If use `np.fft.irfft(fa * fp)` corresponds to a*p. We want a correlated with p at positive lag j: ∑ a[i] p[i+j] = (a * p_rev?)? Let's use a_rev method earlier: conv(a_rev, p)[n-1+j] = g[j]. We can do:
```
a_rev = a[::-1].copy()
fa = np.fft.rfft(a_rev, size_g)
fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size_g)
conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fp, size_g)
g = conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check: a_rev[i]=a[n-1-i]. conv[m]=∑_i a[n-1-i] p[m-i]. m=n-1+j: ∑ a[n-1-i] p[j+n-1-i]. Let t=n-1-i => i=n-1-t; p[j+t] a[t]. yes sum_t a[t]p[j+t]. good. Need length conv = n+2n-1-1=3n-2. size_g >= that. Slice length n: indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive -> 2n-1 length? Wait n-1 + n = 2n-1 exclusive, so length n. Good.
Alternative use `np.fft.rfft(a, size_g)` with p? If use conv p * a_rev? same.

Self conv size_c >=2n-1. `np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size_c)`.

Need ensure nonnegative c: due FFT negative tiny, set `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`. Also if C very small, handle.

But final score from fast_score may differ due FFT rounding vs np.convolve. evaluate_sequence uses np.convolve direct (O(n^2)!) But our fast_score uses FFT; slight differences. def _fast_fft_len(n):
```
    # next power of 2 maybe
    p=1
    while p<n: p*=2
    return p
```

`conv_self_fft(a)`:
```
size = _fft_len(2*len(a)-1)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
return c[:2*len(a)-1]
```
Irfft might produce tiny negative at end; clip.

`cross_g(a,p)`:
```
n=len(a); size=_fft_len(3*n-2)
ar=a[::-1].copy()
fa=np.fft.rfft(ar,size); fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fp,size)
g=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
# maybe clip small negative? g nonnegative? p,a nonnegative, so g>=0; but FFT round small negative; set 0.
g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
return g
```

`smooth_w_from_a(a, beta)`: (a normalized but can be any)
```
c=conv_self(a); C=max; if C<=0 return inf
t=beta*(c-C)/C; t-=t.max(); sumexp=exp(t).sum(); return C + (C/beta)*log(sumexp)
```
For line search from x: compute softmax and smooth_w.

`score_from_x(x,beta)`:
```
xm=x.max(); a=np.exp(x-xm); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return inf; a/=s; w=smooth_w_from_a(a,beta); return w
```
Since softmax normalization, x recenter not needed but exp stable.

`seq_fast_score(seq)`:
```
a=np.asarray(seq,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-15 return inf; c=conv_self; return 2*len(a)*c.max()/(s*s)
```
For scale invariance, no need normalize.

This is like choosing a polynomial C with coefficients ≤1 and nonnegative such that its square root has nonnegative coefficients. We can optimize C via gradient on A; our algorithm does. Maybe can get below 1.502.

Can we use a sequence based on `a_i = c_i c_{n-1-i}`? Product of square-root with its reverse? Let's test. A_i = C_i * C_{n-1-i} (symmetric). Sum? This is like squared? A^2 = C^2 * reverse C^2? Might have more flat? Symmetric sequences often energy high; but product might not. Symmetric A => c_{n-1}=∑a_i² may be large. Probably bad.

What about A_i = C_i - γ C_{i+1}? Nonnegative if γ<~0.5. This modifies early coefficients. Could increase sum? For small γ, maybe. We can include a family: a_i = C_i + α C_{i+1} + β C_{i+2}, positive, tuned. Since C decays slowly, this approximates a smoothed shifted shape. Maybe can improve tail. We can grid α,β for square-root. But gradient can find.

What about using C_i of a fractional integral of order α not 1/2? We analyzed α>1/2 bad infinite but finite maybe? Let's compute small n α<1/2 maybe gives better? For p<0.5, sum grows faster for finite n but max tail? The power profile p<0.5 decays slower, c_k may rise at large k. For finite n, maybe can fill tail and reduce max. Let's test n=10 with a_i=(i+1)^-p, unnormalized. For p=0.25, values: 1,0.84,0.75,0.71,0.69,0.67,0.65,0.64,0.63,0.62 sum~7.0. Convolution max? Early c0=1,c1=2*0.84=1.68,c2≈2*0.75+0.705=2.2; peak early maybe at k~? Could be >1. Normalize max to1 reduces score maybe? Score=2n/S²/M. Unnorm M maybe 3, S²49, score=20*3/49=1.22! Wait if true, power p=0.25 could beat target! Let's calculate more accurately. a_i=(i+1)^-0.25. Values roughly:

- Try 100 block lognormal like previous but in log space: random contiguous block multiply by exp(N(0,σ)). Normalize. Fast score.

Let's draft code mentally:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _fft_len(n):
    p=1
    while p<n: p <<=1
    return p

def _conv_self(a):
    n=a.size
    if n <= 64:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
        c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
        return c
    size=_fft_len(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
    c[:]=np.maximum(c,0.0) # may modify? c is array
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
`np.maximum` returns new; assign.

`_cross_g(a,p)`: if n maybe small, use direct? For n<=64, could use `np.correlate`? Simpler FFT works, but overhead. Use FFT all.
```
def _cross_g(a,p):
    n=a.size
    size=_fft_len(3*n-2)
    ar=a[::-1].copy()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(ar,size)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fp,size)
    g=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(g,0.0,out=g)
    return g
```
`out=g` works if contiguous slice? g is view maybe; okay. Or `g = np.maximum(g,0.0)`.

`_softmax_x(x)`:
```
xm=x.max()
a=np.exp(x-xm)
s=a.sum()
if s<=0: return a/a.size
return a/s
```
`_fast_score(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
a=np.maximum(a,0)
s=a.sum()
if s<1e-15: return np.inf
c=_conv_self(a)
m=c.max() if c.size else 0
if m<=0: return 0.0? But sum positive gives m>0
return float(2*a.size*m/(s*s))
```
Score for all zero inf; but not.

`_smooth_w_gamma(a, gamma)`:
```
c=_conv_self(a); C=float(c.max()); if C<=0 return inf
t=gamma*(c-C)
t=t-t.max()
se=np.exp(t).sum(); if se<=0 return inf
return C + np.log(se)/gamma
```
Use gamma>0.

`_smooth_w_gamma_x(x, gamma)`:
```
a=_softmax_x(x); return _smooth_w_gamma(a,gamma)
```

`_sqroot_coeffs(n, shift=0)`:
```
if shift<0 or shift>=n: return None
vals=np.empty(n)
c=1.0
for i in range(n+shift):
    if i>=shift: vals[i-shift]=c
    if i < n+shift-1:
        c *= (2.0*i + 1.0)/(2.0*i + 2.0)
return vals
```
But for large n, loop in Python 6121 ok. For multiple shifts, recomputes. Could optimize by generating once up to max n+shift for each n; but fine.

`_normalize(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s; return a
```

Optimize:
```
def _state_priority(s):
   return s['score'] + 0.002 * s['stale']

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
   rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
   deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
   cands=_make_candidates()
   best_a, best_score = min(cands...)
   # ensure x for each top
   for s in top:
      a=s['a']; x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); x-=x.max(); x=np.clip(x,-700,0); s['x']=x; s['stale']=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
      top.sort(key=_state_priority)
      s=top[0]
      n=s['a'].size
      rem=deadline - time.monotonic() - 5
      if rem < 20: break
      slice=max(20.0, min(300.0 if n>=2000 else 120.0, rem))
      # maybe if current best score <1.503, longer? ok
      prev=s['score']
      new_a,new_score,new_x, beta0 = _optimize_fixed(s['a'], s['x'], slice, seed=int(rng.integers(0,1e9)), beta0=s.get('beta0',20.0))
      s['a']=new_a; s['score']=new_score; s['x']=new_x
      if new_score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=new_score; best_a=new_a.copy(); s['stale']=0
      elif new_score < prev - 1e-12:
          s['stale']=0
      else:
          s['stale']=s.get('stale',0)+1
   # final validate candidates top few with evaluate
   candidates_final=[best_a]
   for s in top[:5]: candidates_final.append(s['a'])
   # also include analytic best? best_a already.
   final_a=best_a; final_score=np.inf
   for a in candidates_final:
       try:
           sc=evaluate_sequence([float(max(0,min(1000,x))) for x in np.asarray(a).ravel()])
       except Exception:
           sc=np.inf
       if sc < final_score:
           final_score=sc; final_a=a
   # fallback if inf
   if not np.isfinite(final_score):
       # return square-root 6121 or uniform
       n=6121; a=_sqroot_coeffs(n,0); a=a/a.sum(); final_a=a
   return [float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in np.asarray(final_a).ravel()]
```
Need define `_optimize_fixed` signature. We used x0.

```
def _optimize_fixed(a0, x0, time_budget, seed=0, beta0=20.0, target_score=None):
   a=np.asarray(a0,float).copy(); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: a=np.ones(a.size); s=1
   a=a/s
   n=a.size
   if x0 is None or len(x0)!=n:
      x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); x-=x.max(); x=np.clip(x,-700,0)
   else: x=np.asarray(x0,float).copy(); x-=x.max(); x=np.clip(x,-700,0)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(a); best_x=x.copy()
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); mu=0.85; notch=1.0
   lr_target=0.05
   deadline=time.monotonic()+time_budget
   iterations=0
   beta=beta0
   no_improve=0
   last_best=best_score
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       a=_softmax_x(x)
       c=_conv_self(a)
       C=float(c.max())
       if C<=0 or not np.isfinite(C): break
       gamma=beta/max(C,1e-300)
       t=gamma*(c-C); t=t-t.max(); se=np.exp(t).sum()
       if se<=0: break
       W=C + np.log(se)/gamma
       p=np.exp(t); p/=se
       E=float(np.dot(p,c))
       g=_cross_g(a,p)
       base=2.0*a*(g-E)
       # maybe remove mean under a? Not needed.
       # base can have huge values if E large? a small.
       n_iter = iterations+1
       m = mu*m + (1-mu)*base
       v = mu*v + (1-mu)*(base*base)
       mhat = m/(1-mu**n_iter)
       vhat = v/(1-mu**n_iter)
       step = mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-15)
       max_step=float(np.max(np.abs(step))) if n else 0
       if max_step>0:
           scale = lr_target / max_step
           # cap scale? If max_step very tiny, scale can huge; but step after scale max=lr_target. OK.
           step = step*scale
       else: scale=1.0
       # line search
       accepted=False
       x_best=x; W_best=W
       for ls in range(6):
           x_try=x - step
           x_try -= x_try.max(); x_try=np.clip(x_try,-700,0)
           W_try=_smooth_w_gamma_x(x_try, gamma)  # uses same gamma
           if np.isfinite(W_try) and W_try < W_best - 1e-14:
               x=x_try; W=W_try; accepted=True; break
           step *= 0.5
       if accepted:
           iterations +=1
           if iterations % 10 ==0:
               a_check=_softmax_x(x); sc=_fast_score(a_check)
               if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best_score=sc; best_a=a_check.copy(); best_x=x.copy(); no_improve=0
               else:
                   no_improve +=1
           # adapt lr_target and beta
           if no_improve > 80:
               # perturb
               x += 0.05 * np.random.default_rng((seed+iterations)).standard_normal(n) ??? Creating rng each time expensive.
               # Use a local rng defined.
               x -= x.max(); x=np.clip(x,-700,0)
               lr_target *=0.6
               m *=0.5; v*=0.5
               no_improve=0
           if iterations % 100 ==0 and beta < 200:
               beta *=1.25
           if target_score is not None and best_score <= target_score:
               break
       else:
           # fail after backtracking, reduce target and reset
           lr_target *=0.5
           m*=0.5; v*=0.5
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 200:
              break
       if not np.isfinite(W): break
   # At end, maybe final exact from x
   a=_softmax_x(x); sc=_fast_score(a); if sc<best_score: best_a=a; best_score=sc; best_x=x
   return best_a,best_score,best_x,beta
```

```
   top=[]; cnt={}
   for s in cands:
      n=s['a'].size
      key=n
      if cnt.get(key,0) >= 3: continue
      top.append(s); cnt[key]=cnt.get(key,0)+1
      if len(top)>=max_states: break
   # ensure given length present if any
   if given_n is not None and all(s['a'].size != given_n for s in top):
      # find candidate with length given_n (if top full, replace worst)
      for s in cands:
         if s['a'].size == given_n:
            if len(top)<max_states: top.append(s)
            else:
               top[-1]=s
            break
   return top
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a)**2).

The method combines several closed-form / analytic near-optimal shapes
(square-root of 1/(1-z), tilted and rescaled variants) with a fast
FFT-based smoothed-max local optimizer in log-coordinates.  Candidate
lengths are explored, the most promising states are refined in time-sliced
rounds, and the best evaluated sequence is returned.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _fft_len(n):
    """Next power of two >= n."""
    p = 1
    while p < n:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _conv_self(a, size=None):
    """Linear self-convolution, clipped to nonnegative."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 64:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        return c
    if size is None:
        size = _fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def _cross_g(a, p, size=None):
    """
    g[j] = sum_i a[i] * p[i+j] for j = 0..n-1,
    where p has length 2n-1.  Computed via one linear convolution.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if size is None:
        size = _fft_len(3 * n - 2)
    ar = a[::-1].copy()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, size)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fp, size)
    g = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return g


def _softmax_x(x):
    """Softmax of log-coordinates, always returns a finite simplex point."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.zeros_like(x)
    xm = float(np.max(x))
    a = np.exp(x - xm)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full_like(x, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a /= s
    return a


def _fast_score(a):
    """Score 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2, using FFT when helpful."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_self(a)
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _smooth_w_gamma(a, gamma, size=None):
    """
    Homogeneous smooth max of conv(a,a):
        C + (1/gamma) * log sum exp(gamma*(c-C)), C=max(c).
    """
    if gamma <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(gamma):
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_self(a, size)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    C = float(np.max(c))
    if C <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(C):
        return np.inf
    t = gamma * (c - C)
    t = t - float(np.max(t))
    se = float(np.sum(np.exp(t)))
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        return np.inf
    return float(C + np.log(se) / gamma)


def _smooth_w_gamma_x(x, gamma, size=None):
    """Smooth objective for log-coordinates x."""
    a = _softmax_x(x)
    return _smooth_w_gamma(a, gamma, size)


def _sqroot_coeffs(n, shift=0):
    """
    Coefficients binom(2k,k)/4^k, starting at index shift, length n.
    For shift=0, the first n convolution coefficients are exactly constant.
    """
    if n <= 0 or shift < 0 or shift >= n:
        return None
    vals = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = 1.0
    total = n + shift
    for i in range(total):
        if i >= shift:
            vals[i - shift] = c
        if i < total - 1:
            c *= (2.0 * i + 1.0) / (2.0 * i + 2.0)
    return vals


def _add_candidate(cands, a):
    """Normalize nonnegative vector, score it, and append if finite."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return
    a = a / s
    sc = _fast_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        cands.append({"a": a, "score": float(sc)})


def _state_key(st):
    return st["score"]


def _state_priority(st):
    return float(st["score"]) + 0.0015 * float(st.get("stale", 0))


def _select_top(cands, max_states=12):
    """Pick diverse low-score candidate states."""
    if not cands:
        return []
    cands = sorted(cands, key=_state_key)
    top = []
    cnt = {}
    for st in cands:
        n = int(st["a"].size)
        if cnt.get(n, 0) >= 3:
            continue
        top.append(st)
        cnt[n] = cnt.get(n, 0) + 1
        if len(top) >= max_states:
            break
    return top


def _make_initial_states():
    cands = []
    given_len = None

    # Provided state-of-the-art sequence, if available.
    try:
        init = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        given_len = int(init.size)
        _add_candidate(cands, init)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # A set of lengths: the supplied length, larger useful lengths, and
    # shorter lengths that can sometimes have better finite-size behavior.
    lengths = [
        6121, 4500, 3000, 2200, 1600, 1200, 900, 700, 500,
        350, 250, 200, 150, 120, 100, 80, 60, 40, 30, 20, 15, 10, 8, 6, 5
    ]
    if given_len is not None and given_len not in lengths:
        lengths.insert(0, int(given_len))

    # Plain shifted square-root shapes.
    for n in lengths:
        if n <= 0:
            continue
        for shift in (0, 1, 2, 4, 8):
            if shift >= n:
                continue
            vals = _sqroot_coeffs(int(n), shift)
            if vals is not None and np.sum(vals) > 0:
                _add_candidate(cands, vals)

    # Tilted/rescaled variants of the best analytic shape.
    tilt_lengths = [6121, 3000, 1600, 1000, 700, 500, 300, 200, 100, 50, 20, 10]
    for n in tilt_lengths:
        if n <= 1:
            continue
        base = _sqroot_coeffs(int(n), 0)
        if base is None or np.sum(base) <= 0:
            continue
        if base.size > 1:
            u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, base.size)
            centered = u - 0.5
            for t in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                try:
                    _add_candidate(cands, base * np.exp(t * centered))
                except Exception:
                    pass
        for q in (0.75, 1.25):
            try:
                _add_candidate(cands, np.power(base, q))
            except Exception:
                pass

    # A few random simplex points for diversity.
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    random_lengths = [1000, 500, 200, 100, 40, 12]
    for n in random_lengths:
        for _ in range(2):
            a = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.05
            a *= np.exp(0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n) * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n))
            _add_candidate(cands, a)

    top = _select_top(cands, max_states=12)

    # Ensure the supplied length is represented if it exists.
    if given_len is not None and all(int(st["a"].size) != given_len for st in top):
        for st in cands:
            if int(st["a"].size) == given_len:
                if len(top) < 12:
                    top.append(st)
                else:
                    top[-1] = st
                break

    if not top:
        a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        top = [{"a": a, "score": float(_fast_score(a))}]

    # Store initial log-coordinates for each state.
    for st in top:
        a = st["a"]
        x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        x -= float(np.max(x))
        x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
        st["x"] = x
        st["stale"] = 0
        st["beta0"] = 20.0
    return top


def _try_perturbations(x, best_x, best_a, best_score, rng, attempts=24):
    """Cheap random restarts around the current log-state."""
    n = int(x.size)
    improved = False

    # Full-vector log noise.
    half = max(1, attempts // 2)
    for _ in range(half):
        x_try = x + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_try)):
            continue
        x_try -= float(np.max(x_try))
        x_try = np.clip(x_try, -700.0, 0.0)
        a_try = _softmax_x(x_try)
        sc = _fast_score(a_try)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            improved = True
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            best_x = x_try.copy()

    # Blockwise log perturbations.
    for _ in range(half):
        x_try = best_x.copy()
        min_len = max(1, n // 300)
        max_len = min(n, max(2, n // 10))
        ln = int(rng.integers(min_len, max_len + 1))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - ln + 1)))
        if st + ln > n:
            ln = n - st
        if ln <= 0:
            continue
        x_try[st:st + ln] += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_try)):
            continue
        x_try -= float(np.max(x_try))
        x_try = np.clip(x_try, -700.0, 0.0)
        a_try = _softmax_x(x_try)
        sc = _fast_score(a_try)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            improved = True
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            best_x = x_try.copy()

    if improved:
        return best_x.copy(), best_a, best_score, best_x.copy(), True
    return x, best_a, best_score, best_x, False


def _optimize_fixed(a0, x0, time_budget, seed=0, beta0=20.0, target_score=None):
    """
    Local optimizer for one fixed length.

    Works in log-coordinates for a simplex point a=softmax(x).  It minimizes
    a FFT-based smooth maximum of conv(a,a).  The objective is scale
    invariant because the simplex normalization is implicit.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    n = int(a.size)

    if x0 is None:
        x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    else:
        x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
        if x.size != n:
            x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    x -= float(np.max(x))
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(_fast_score(a))
    best_x = x.copy()

    size_c = _fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    size_g = _fft_len(3 * n - 2)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_budget))

    mu = 0.90
    nu = 0.999
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(max(5.0, beta0))
    lr_target = 0.08
    it = 0
    stale = 0
    perturb_count = 0
    max_perturbs = 12

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        a = _softmax_x(x)
        c = _conv_self(a, size_c)
        C = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if C <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(C):
            break

        # Keep smoothing degree relative to current peak: gamma*C = beta.
        gamma = beta / max(C, 1e-300)

        t = gamma * (c - C)
        t = t - float(np.max(t))
        exp_t = np.exp(t)
        se = float(np.sum(exp_t))
        if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
            break
        p = exp_t / se
        E = float(np.dot(p, c))
        g = _cross_g(a, p, size_g)
        base = 2.0 * a * (g - E)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(base)):
            base = np.nan_to_num(base, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        it += 1
        m = mu * m + (1.0 - mu) * base
        v = nu * v + (1.0 - nu) * (base * base)
        b1 = 1.0 - mu ** it
        b2 = 1.0 - nu ** it
        mhat = m / b1
        vhat = v / b2
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-15)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(step)):
            step = np.nan_to_num(step, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        max_step = float(np.max(np.abs(step))) if n else 0.0
        if max_step > 0.0:
            step *= lr_target / max_step
        else:
            step = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        W_cur = _smooth_w_gamma(a, gamma, size_c)
        accepted = False
        for _ in range(6):
            x_try = x - step
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_try)):
                step *= 0.5
                continue
            x_try -= float(np.max(x_try))
            x_try = np.clip(x_try, -700.0, 0.0)
            W_try = _smooth_w_gamma_x(x_try, gamma, size_c)
            if np.isfinite(W try_skip := 0):
                pass
            if np.isfinite(W_try) and (
                W_try < W_cur - 1e-15
                or (W_try < W_cur + 1e-12 and np.linalg.norm(step) > 0)
            ):
                x = x_try
                accepted = True
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            lr_target = max(0.01, lr_target * 0.6)
            m *= 0.5
            v *= 0.5
            stale += 1
            if stale > 80:
                if perturb_count < max_perturbs:
                    x, best_a, best_score, best_x, ok = _try_perturbations(
                        x, best_x, best_a, best_score, rng, attempts=24
                    )
                    if ok:
                        perturb_count += 1
                        stale = 0
                else:
                    break
            continue

        # Periodic exact/fast score check and saving.
        if it % 8 == 0:
            a_check = _softmax_x(x)
            sc_check = _fast_score(a_check)
            if np.isfinite(sc_check) and sc_check < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = float(sc_check)
                best_a = a_check.copy()
                best_x = x.copy()
                stale = 0
            else:
                stale += 1

        # Adapt smoothing and step scale.
        if it % 120 == 0:
            if beta < 350.0:
                beta *= 1.25
            if stale == 0 and lr_target < 0.35:
                lr_target *= 1.08
            elif stale > 40:
                lr_target *= 0.7

        if stale > 90:
            x, best_a, best_score, best_x, ok = _try_perturbations(
                x, best_x, best_a, best_score, rng, attempts=32
            )
            if ok:
                perturb_count += 1
                stale = 0
                m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                # Sync to best state and reduce search radius.
                x = best_x.copy()
                lr_target *= 0.6
                m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        if target_score is not None and best_score <= float(target_score):
            break

    # Final exact/fast refinement from current x.
    a = _softmax_x(x)
    sc = _fast_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = float(sc)
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_x = x.copy()

    return best_a, float(best_score), best_x, float(beta)


def _validated_final_sequence(candidate, known_best):
    """Validate with the official evaluator when possible."""
    final_a = None
    final_score = np.inf
    seen = set()

    for a in candidate:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            continue
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        key = (int(arr.size), float(np.sum(np.maximum(arr, 0.0))))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        try:
            seq = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, z))) for z in arr]
            sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
            final_score = float(sc)
            final_a = seq
    if final_a is not None:
        return final_a
    return [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, z))) for z in known_best]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point.  Uses the supplied wall-clock budget and returns the best
    nonnegative coefficient list found.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, total - 8.0)

    states = _make_initial_states()
    global_best_a = min(states, key=_state_key)["a"].copy()
    global_best_score = float(global_best_a_scores := _fast_score(global_best_a))

    # Main loop: repeatedly refine the currently most promising state.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        states.sort(key=_state_priority)
        st = states[0]
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0
        if rem < 20.0:
            break

        n = int(st["a"].size)
        if n >= 2500:
            chunk = min(420.0, rem)
        elif n >= 400:
            chunk = min(220.0, rem)
        else:
            chunk = min(90.0, rem)
        chunk = max(20.0, chunk)

        seed_i = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
        new_a, new_score, new_x, beta = _optimize_fixed(
            st["a"], st["x"], chunk, seed=seed_i, beta0=st.get("beta0", 20.0),
            target_score=global_best_score
        )

        prev_score = float(st["score"])
        st["a"] = new_a
        st["x"] = new_x
        st["beta0"] = beta
        st["score"] = float(new_score)

        if abs(new_score - prev_score) > 1e-12:
            st["stale"] = 0
        else:
            st["stale"] = int(st.get("stale", 0)) + 1

        if new_score < global_best_score - 1e-12:
            global_best_score = float(new_score)
            global_best_a = new_a.copy()

    # Final validation candidates: global best plus a few top states.
    states.sort(key=_state_priority)
    final_candidates = [global_best_a]
    for st in states[:5]:
        final_candidates.append(st["a"])

    # If everything looks bad, fall back to a safe analytic sequence.
    fallback = _sqroot_coeffs(6121, 0)
    if fallback is None or np.sum(fallback) <= 0:
        fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    fallback = fallback / float(np.sum(fallback))
    final_candidates.append(fallback)

    return _validated_final_sequence(final_candidates, global_best_a)
```
